Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場
或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

1

2301 光寶科技(Lite-On)

報 告 日 期 ：2026 /08/03 研 究 員： 吳 和善  (Jason Wu ) 
壹、投 資 建 議 
一. 2Q26 稅後 EPS 為 3.14 元， YoY+126%  
2Q26 合 併 營 收 達 新 台 幣527 億元 ，YoY +30% 、QoQ +21% ， 主 要 為 
部 分 高 階 產 品 遞 延 至2Q26 出 貨，毛 利 率 則 跳 升 至27.22% ，主 要 受
惠 1Q26AI 高 階 電 源 遞 延 出 貨 所 致，費 用 率 因 營 收 規 模 增 而 略 降 至
11.66% ，2Q26 稅後 EPS 為 3.14 元， YoY +126% 。

二. AI 產品營收占比將達 30% ，HVDC 將小量產  
展望 3Q26 ， 公 司 預 計 核 心 事 業 將 展 現 強 勁 之 年 增 與 季 增 動 能 ， 下
半 年 整 體 表 現 將 優 於 上 半 年，由於 AI 產 品 占 比 已 於 2Q26 突破
25% ， 其 中 電 源 約15% 、BBU 儲能 10% ， 全 年 度 AI 產 品 占 比 將 達
30% 。在 電 源 部 分，新 一 代 110kW Power Shelf 、8.5kW PSU 及 BBU
等 於 下 半 年 陸 續 進 入 驗 證 與 生 產 階 段有 助 於 產 品 結 構 持 續 轉 佳 ，
再加上公司主要客戶將 BBU 視為標配品因此在後續擴大
PSU+BBU 等 訂 單 規 劃 ， 屆 時 有 助 公 司 逐 步 整 合 產 品 ， 再 加 上 公 司
已 有 新 切 入 CSP 客 戶 ， 將 逐 步 從 電 源 開 始 導 入BBU 等 整 合 性 產
品；HVDC 部 分，目 前已 在 進 行 送 樣 測 試 預 期11 月 將 有 小 量 生 產，
但 整 體 放 量時 間 點約在 1Q27 ~2Q27 。

三. 預估 2026 年 EPS 為 9.91 元，YoY+51%  
全年 AI 營 收 占 比 將 達30% ，非 AI 事 業 則 以 低 軌 衛 星 電 源 較 為 強
勁 但 部 分 業 務 將 因 客 戶 缺 料 而 影 響 營 收 動 能，預 估2026 年 營 收 為
2,053 億 元，YoY +23.6% 。毛 利 率 部 分 因1H26 毛 利 率 接 近25% 且下
半 年 產 品 優 化 下 將 有 助 於 獲 利 結 構 轉 佳 ， 故 上 調 全 年 毛 利 率 為
25.01 %， 費 用 率 略 降 至12.2%，預估 2026 年 稅 後 EPS+ 51% YoY 至
9.91 元。 2027 年 在 新 平 台 電 源 皆 提 升 至10kW 以 上 再 加 上 HVDC
產 品 開 始 量 產 後 將 推 升AI 相 關 營 收 動 能 ， 預 估2027 年 營 收 為2,480
億 元 ，YoY+ 20.7% ， 毛 利 率 受 惠AI 營 收 占 比 提 升 故 使 得 毛 利 率 升
至 25.27%、 費 用 率11.68%，2027 年稅後 EPS 為 12.33 元，YoY+24%。

四. 維持 買進，目標價 296 元 
全 球 市 場 對AI 電 源 及 相 關 產 品 需 求 強 勁， 公 司 可 提 供 產 品 或 系 統
化 解 決 方 案 持 續 帶 動整 體 獲 利 結 構 持 續 優 化，在 2Q26 獲 利 優 於 預
期 且 切 入 新 客 戶 後 助AI 營 收 占 比 提 升 帶 動獲 利 成 長 ，故 維 持 買 進
建 議 ，上修 目 標 價至 296 元（24X 2027 年 稅 後 EPS ）。

投資評等：買進 
目標價位：296 元 
      24X2027EPS 
參考價位：209 元 
      17X2027EPS 
     2026/07/31 收盤

貳、公 司 概 況 
一. 公司簡介

光 寶 科(2301.TT) 創立於  1975 年 ， 位 於 新 北 市 中 和 區 ， 為 全 球 光 電
元 件 及 電 子 關 鍵 模 組 之 領 導 廠 商，亦 為 台 灣 第 一 家 上 市 之 電 子 公
司，最早以  LED 為主要產品，後來切入電腦電源供應器， LED 、
半 導 體、電 腦 機 殼、影 像 產 品、主 機 板、DVD 及 光 碟 設 備、固 態 硬
碟 、 以 及 其 他 電 子 零 組 件

光 寶 科 的 事 業 分 類 為 資 訊 及 消 費 電 子 、 雲 端 運 算 及 物 聯 網 、 光 電 
三 大 部 門，2025 年 營 收 佔 比 分 別 為 38%、45%、17%，其 中 雲 端 運
算 及 物 聯 網 、 光 電 部 門 為 營 收 獲 利 成 長 主 力， 顯 示 公 司 產 品 結 構
持 續 轉 佳。

表 一 、 公 司 部 門 及 產 品 內 容

資 料 來 源 ：光 寶 科

以 2025 年 營 運 狀 況 來 看 ， 年 營 收+21% YoY 至 1,661 億 元 ， 毛 利 率
為 22.9% 、 稅 後 EPS 為 6.64 元， YoY+27% 。 公 司 亦 公 告 配 發 現 金
股 利 全 年 配 發5 元 ， 配 發 率75% 。

二. 公司 營運概況與展望

1. 2Q26 稅後 EPS 為 3.14 元，YoY+126%  
2Q26 合 併 營 收 達 新 台 幣527 億元 ，YoY +30% 、QoQ +21% ， 主 要 為 
部 分 高 階 產 品 遞 延 至2Q26 出貨，毛 利 率 則 跳 升 至27.22% ，主 要 受
惠 1Q26AI 高 階 電 源 遞 延 出 貨 所 致。產 品 結 構 部 分，雲 端 及 物 聯 網
部門  (Cloud/AIoT) 占 總 營 收 比 重 升 至55% ， 其 中 營 收+26%QoQ 但
營利 +102%QoQ 顯 示 規 模 與AI 產 品 結 構 對 獲 利 貢 獻 程 度 。費用率
因 營 收 規 模 增 而 略 降 至11.66% ，2Q26 稅後 EPS 為 3.14 元， YoY 
+126% 。

2. AI 產 品 營 收 占 比 將 達30% ，HVDC 將 小 量 產 
展望 3Q26 ， 公 司 預 計 核 心 事 業 將 展 現 強 勁 之 年 增 與 季 增 動 能 ， 下

25% ， 其 中電 源 約 15% 、BBU 儲能 10% ， 全 年 度 AI 產 品 占 比 將 達
30%。 在 電 源 部 分 ，新一代 110kW Power Shelf 、8.5kW PSU 配合 BBU
於 下 半 年 陸 續 進 入 驗 證 與 生 產 階 段有 助 於 產 品 結 構 持 續 轉 佳 ， 再
加 上 公 司 主 要 客 戶 將BBU 視 為 標 配 品 因 此 在 後 續 擴 大PSU+BBU
等 訂 單 規 劃 ， 屆 時 有 助 公 司 逐 步 整 合 產 品 ， 再 加 上 公 司 已 有 新 切
入 CSP 客 戶 ， 將 逐 步 從 電 源 開 始 導 入BBU 等 整 合 性 產 品 ；HVDC
部 分，目 前 已 在 進 行 送 樣 測 試 預 期11 月 將 有 小 量 生 產，但 整 體 放
量時間 ASIC 客 戶 落在 1Q27 、GPU 客 戶 則在 2Q27 。

3. 獲 利 結 構 有 望 持 續 轉 佳 ，持續 布局未來 商機  
2Q26 毛 利 率 高 達 27.2% 主 要 有 產 品 遞 延 出 貨 及 部 分 一 次 性 因 素 ，
若以 1H26 來 看 毛 利 率 達24.7% 仍 較 過 往 高。展 望2H26，毛 利 率 將
有 望 維 持 在 靠 近25% ， 其 中 除 了 電 源 規 格 提 升 之 外 而BBU 也 隨 規
格 提 升 搭 配 出 貨，而 2H26 各 家 廠 商 皆 面 臨 原 物 料 及 零 組 件 漲 價 的
情 況 ， 公 司 也 提 前 備 好 下 半 年 產 品 出 貨 用 料 拉 長 庫 存 期 間 或 藉 由
新 產 品 來 調 整 產 品 售 價 ， 在 整 體 獲 利 結 構 上 應 不 至 於 影 響 太 大 ；
隨 公 司 由 單 一 產 品 轉 為 系 統 解 決 方 案 後，公 司 持 續 擴 大 整 合 版 圖， 
其 中 包 含 剛 通 過 董 事 會投資 3,430 萬 美 元 取 得 新 加 坡 光 通 信 元 件
廠 DenseLight Photonics （ 布 局 光 通 訊 與 CPO ） ， 對 公 司 現 有 光 學
封 裝 將 進 一 步 整 合 至 上 游 磊 晶 生 產 製 造 ； 除 此 之 外 ， 公 司 亦 擴 大
投 資 ， 並 將 原 本2026 年 資 本 支 出130 億 元 上 修 至180 億 元 ， 主 要
針 對 大 電 力 設 備 進 行 建 置 以 著 眼 後 續HVDC 商 機 ， 而 除高 雄 二 期
與 越 南 廣 寧 廠 一 期 分 別 於3Q26 及 4Q26 正 式 量 產， 將 持 續 擴 增 飛
中 國 產 能 。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

一. 預估 2026 年 EPS 為 9.91 元，YoY+51% 
展望 2026 年 ， 由 於 AI 客 戶 需 求 持 續 旺 盛 ， 舊 客 戶 需 求 拉 升 以 及
有 望 開 發 新 客 戶，擴 大 電 源、BBU 及 散 熱 產 品，全年 AI 營 收 占 比
將達 30%，非 AI 事業 則 以 低 軌 衛 星 電 源 較 為 強 勁 但 部 分 業 務 將 因
客戶缺料而影響營收動能 ，預估 2026 年營收 為 2,053 億元，
YoY +23.6 %。毛 利 率 部 分 因1H26 毛 利 率 接 近25% 且 下 半 年 產 品 優
化 下 將 有 助 於 獲 利 結 構 轉 佳 ， 故 上 調全 年 毛 利 率 為25.01 %， 費 用
因 持 續 擴 增 產 線 規 模 及 研 發因此 費用 年增 17% 、 費 用 率 略 降至
12.2%，預估 2026 年 稅 後 EPS+ 51%YoY 至 9.91 元 。2027 年 在 新 平
台 電 源 皆 提 升 至10kW 以 上 再 加 上HVDC 產 品 開 始 量 產 後 將 推 升
AI 相 關 營 收 動 能 ， 預 估2027 年 營 收 為 2,480 億元， YoY+ 20.7% ，
毛 利 率 受 惠 AI 營 收 占 比 提 升 故 使 得 毛 利 率 升 至25.27% 、 費 用 率
11.68% ，2027 年 稅 後 EPS 為 12.33 元， YoY+24% 。

2308 台達電(Delta)

報告日期： 2026/07/31 研 究 員：吳和善  (Jason Wu) 
壹、投 資 建 議 
一. 2Q26 稅後 EPS 為 9.68 元，YoY+80% 
2Q26 合併營收為 1,832 億元，YoY+48%、QoQ+15%，其中電源及
零組件營收年增 50%、占比達 54%。34%的基礎設施年增 78%，其
動能主要受惠於散熱基期較低、2Q26 貢獻已達 10% 。2Q26 毛利率
為 35.6% 、 費用率略低於 19% ， 2Q26 稅後 EPS 為 9.68 元 ， YoY+80% 。

二. AI 電源與散熱將持續推升 2026 年營運動能 
現階段公司 AI 電源營收已超過 25%、散熱液冷產品達 10%，預期
2H26 仍維持強勁營運動能。展望 2H26 ，電源年成長相對高於由於
液冷產品；HVDC 部分，3Q26 將有 小量出貨，其中由於 400VDC
為直接對 CSP 出貨因此量相對較多，但整體明年才有顯著放量。

三. 調整供貨及成本結構亦持續擴增產能因應 
2026 年起原物料及相關電子零組件皆呈現漲價情況 ， 從 2Q26 開始
有受到成本上漲影響，因此開始針對合約與客戶進行協商預期有
望在 1~2 季度陸續開始反應售價以保有合理毛利率；而在資本支
出部分，2025 年資本支出達 466 億元，預期全年將達 700 億元。

四. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 為 39.57 元，YoY+71% 
預估 2026 年營收將達 7,603 億元，YoY+37% 。由於 2Q26 毛利不如
預期，考量將受成本提升影響抵銷部分 2H26 營運規模效益，故有
下修全年毛利率至 36.13%，費用持續因擴大投資、整體費用率降
至 17.95%，上修 2026 年全年稅後 EPS 為 39.57 元，YoY+71%。

五. 預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 為 65.61 元，YoY+60% 
預估 2027 年營收為 1.082 兆元，YoY+42%，毛利率略降至 36.1%。
由於公司持續挹注資本投入故有上修費用支出但因經濟規模使得
費用率降至 16%、營利率則升至 20%，2027 年稅後 EPS 為 65.61
元，YoY+60%。

六. 維持買進，目標價 2,300 元 
AI 建置需求持續成長且公司已具備關鍵產業地位，且在整合多項
業務後已提升至解決方案整合商，投資建議維持買進，惟美國 Fed
升息機率提高將不利評價故有下修本益比，目標價為 2,300 元。
(35X2027 年稅後 EPS)

貳、公 司 概 況

一. 公司簡介  
台達電 (2308) 成立於創立於  1971 年 ， 過往台達電專注於IT 相關的
電源供應器及風扇，包括 NB、DT、Monitor 、Server 、Printer 及網通
類產品，因全球電子產業的轉變，及環保意識的抬頭，台達電近
年積極跨入工業自動化、智慧安防及綠能領域。近年來，台達集
團已逐步從關鍵零組件製造商邁入整體節能解決方案提供者，深
耕「電源及零組件」 、「自動化」與「基礎設施」三大業務範疇，
為全球提供電源管理與散熱解決方案的轉業廠商，在面對日益嚴
重的氣候變遷，公司秉持「環保  節能  愛地球」的經營使命，持
續開發創新節能產品及解決方案、致力於提升產品的能源轉換效
率，減輕全球暖化對人類生存的衝擊。

圖一、台達電解決方案產品

資料來源：台達電

二. 公司營運概況與展望 
1.2Q26 稅後 EPS 為 9.68 元，YoY+80% 
2Q26 合併營收為 1,832 億元，YoY+48%、QoQ+15%，成長動能主
要來自於 AI 強勁需求帶動。產品結構部分，電源及零組件營收年
增 50%、占比達 54%，其中主要貢獻來自於伺服器電源。營收占
比達 34%的基礎設施年增 78% ， 其動能主要受惠於散熱基期較低、
2Q26 貢獻已達 10%。其他交通及自動化業務動能較為疲弱且呈現
小幅虧損狀態但不影響整體營運趨勢。2Q26 毛利率為 35.6%低於
1Q26 水準(1Q26 毛利率較高主因一次性銷貨補貼收入及認列條款
影響)部分略受到零件成本影響。費用率略低於 19%，主要因擴大
營運量能提高 RD 費用等，2Q26 稅後 EPS 為 9.68 元，YoY+80%。

2.AI 電源與散熱將持續推升 2026 年營運動能 
雖然近期美國 AI 大廠面臨資本支出及現金流等質疑，不過公司認
為 AI 應用仍持續普及，再加上觀察客戶端資本支出持續成長趨
勢，故認為 AI 建置潮仍將持續，惟過程中仍將持續面臨零組件供
貨、資料中心選址及施工遞延等狀況。以現階段公司 AI 電源營收
已超過 25%、散熱液冷產品達 10%，預期 2H26 仍維持強勁營運動
能。展望 2H26，電源年成長相對高於由於液冷產品(2H25 基期較
高) ； HVDC 部分 ， 公司認為 400VDC 、 800VDC 維持進度 ， 預計 3Q26
起將有小量出貨，其中由於 400VDC 為直接對 CSP 出貨因此量相
對較多，800VDC 因將由 NV 銷售客戶因此仍有待確認上市後狀
況，但整體而言 HVDC 今年僅有小量出貨，明年才有顯著放量。

3.調整供貨及成本結構亦持續擴增產能因應 
2026 年起原物料及相關電子零組件皆呈現漲價情況，公司持續藉
由規模優勢確保料源 ，雖然公司在記憶體使用較少但從 2Q26 開始
有受到成本上漲影響，因此開始針對合約與客戶進行協商預期有
望在 1~2 季度陸續開始反應售價以保有合理毛利率；而在資本支
出部分，2025 年資本支出達 466 億元，1H26 支出達 275 億元，預
期全年將達 700 億元。工廠部分，公司將更新泰國一廠擴增數倍
產能、2027 年再有 3 新廠完工，另仍將在台灣、中國及美國擴增
廠房大樓及 RD 人員以確保未來可跟上客戶 AI 及能源相關需求。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

一. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 為 39.57 元，YoY+71% 
展望 2026 年因 AI 需求持續推升、新產能持續開出，而資本支出

亦高於去年故將持續擴大營收貢獻，再加上公司聚焦整合組裝業
務持續編列整合產線將有助於 AI 營收動能持續強勁成長，預估
2026 年營收將達 7,603 億元，YoY+37%。毛利率部分，由於 2Q26
毛利不如預期 ， 考量將受成本提升影響抵銷部分 2H26 營運規模及
稼動率效益，故有下修全年毛利率至 36.13%，費用持續因擴大投
資、整體費用率降至 17.95%，上修 2026 年全年稅後 EPS 為 39.57
元，YoY+71%。

二. 預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 為 65.61 元，YoY+60% 
隨新一代伺服器上市 2027 年放量帶動電源規格及需求再度提升，
而 HVDC 於 2027 年被納入 AI 資料中心建置後開始放量，由於公
司將以 Power Rack 出整合出貨將放大營收規模，且帶動台達電競
爭優勢，由於公司已有 HVDC 產品再加上整合市電經由資料中心
至 AI 伺服器的應用因此營收規模仍可維持強勁動能，預估 2027
年營收為 1.082 兆元，YoY+42%，毛利率略降至 36.1%。由於公司
持續挹注資本投入故有上修費用支出但因經濟規模使得費用率降
至 16% 、 營利率則升至 20% ， 2027 年稅後 EPS 為 65.61 元 ， YoY+60% 。

陳鈞皓 C00264@ibfs.com.tw 
2360.TT 致茂 
 半導體測試需求強勁 成長動能無虞

[法說會更新]   
   公司簡介 
致茂電子成立於 1984 年，為專業測試儀器領導廠商，提供測光源、電源、半導體設
備等儀器，並以自有品牌 Chroma 行銷全球。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
強力買進 2,700 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】買進，2,700 
【大盤指數/股價】39933 / 1910

➢ 2Q26 營運優於預期 毛利率呈現季度成長 
回顧 2Q26，量測儀器產品線營收約 61.69 億元，佔母公司營收 56%，
與 1Q26 的 58%差異不大。半導體與光電測試營收為 43.86 億元，佔比
則為 39%，與 1Q26 的 37%差異不大。獲利率方面，毛利率自上一季的
62.6%下滑至本季的 60.5%，主因短期受到 IC、被動元件等原物料漲價
所影響 。 另外不同區域別帶來的產品組合差異也使毛利率受到微幅影響 。
而受惠業外因處分凌華股票及認列員工住宅收入使業外貢獻 12.72 億
元，相較 1Q26 的 2.97 億元大幅提升，帶動 EPS 呈現 QoQ 成長。整體
而言 2Q26 本業符合預期，財報受惠業外貢獻而優於預期。2Q26 營收
135.29 億元， QoQ+14.08% ， YoY+109.58% 。 EPS 12.15 元，
QoQ+32.58%，YoY+162.27%。 
➢ 2H26 成長動能集中半導體 FT/SLT Handler 
1H26 成長動能較大來自於量測儀器產品線，以 2Q26 來說該產品線年
增約 129%。而下半年受惠 GPU、CPU、LPU 等晶片對 SLT 需求浮現，
帶動半導體測試產品線有較強的成長動能。而觀察終端晶片量而言美系
GPU 大廠之晶片數量於 2027 年約在 600~700 萬顆之需求，該公司之
CPU 產品則約 500~600 萬顆需求。該部分需求之 SLT 測試機台已於上
半年陸續貢獻營收。下半年則接續美系 A 客戶於 2027 年的需求，尤其
其 CPU 需求量約達 500~600 萬顆，不亞於 GPU 客戶之晶片出貨量，
帶動下半年半導體測試 SLT 機台出貨力道強勁。 
➢ 未來兩年成長路徑明確 股價仍未高估 
電源測試機台於上半年已大幅成長，下半年則由半導體測試機台接棒，
3Q26~4Q26 則有 CPO 相關測試機台陸續貢獻營收 ，2H26~2027 年成
長動能明確 。 考量國際同業如 Teradyne、Advantest 等測試領導廠商在
同樣高成長性下享有超過 40X 之本益比，研究員預期未來兩年為致茂之
高成長期，應享有較高之評價，並預估 2027 年 EPS 約 63.26 元。考量
以上，維持目標價 2,700 元(2027F PE 42x)，建議強力買進。

個股報告

公司介紹 
致茂電子成立於 1984 年，為專業測試儀器領導廠商，提供測光源、電源、半導體設
備等儀器，並以自有品牌 Chroma 行銷全球。公司產品廣度及深度具一定規模，包括
用於電源供應器 、 變壓器 、 充電裝之電源測試機台 。 用於電源管理IC、RF 晶片 、CPU、
GPU 等分選機。及用於 3D 感測器 CPO 矽光等光電整合測試機台等。以產品營收佔
比分類 ，2Q26 量測及自動化佔母公司比率約 56%、 半導體/矽光測試占母公司比例約
39%。 
圖 1：2Q26 產品營收佔比示意圖

資料來源：公司資料 
2Q26 營運優於預期 毛利率呈現季度成長 
回顧 2Q26，量測儀器產品線營收約 61.69 億元，佔母公司營收 56%，與 1Q26 的
58%差異不大。半導體與光電測試營收為 43.86 億元，佔比則為 39%，與 1Q26 的
37%差異不大。獲利率方面，毛利率自上一季的 62.6%下滑至本季的 60.5%，主因短
期受到 IC、被動元件等原物料漲價所影響。另外不同區域別帶來的產品組合差異也使
毛利率受到微幅影響。而受惠業外因處分凌華股票及認列員工住宅收入使業外貢獻
12.72 億元，相較 1Q26 的 2.97 億元大幅提升，帶動 EPS 呈現 QoQ 成長。整體而言
2Q26 本業符合預期，財報受惠業外貢獻而優於預期。 2Q26 營收 135.29 億元，
QoQ+14.08%，YoY+109.58%。EPS 12.15 元，QoQ+32.58%，YoY+162.27%。 
2H26 成長動能集中半導體 FT/SLT Handler 
1H26 成長動能較大來自於量測儀器產品線，以 2Q26 來說該產品線年增約 129%。
而下半年受惠 GPU、CPU、LPU 等晶片對 SLT 需求浮現，帶動半導體測試產品線有較
強的成長動能。半導體測試主要成長動能來自於 SLT 分選機，SLT 測試透過模擬真實
的軟硬體環境來確認晶片功能的正常性 ， 另外公司也支援ATC 控溫之系統 ， 主因GPU
運算時會產生較高的功耗，透過 ATC 可模擬不同環境下之測試環境。而測試機台龍頭
Teradyne(TER US)及 Advantest(6857 JT)皆表示測試機台市場將有大幅之成長。
Teradyne 在最新的電話會議提到 ， 預估在2030 年 ATE 市場規模將超過 200 億美元 ，
相較 2025 年的 120 億美元有 60%以上的增長幅度，且預期 ATE 佔晶圓廠資本支出
比例自 2023 年的 4%提升至 2026 年的 8%，相較過去的佔比有翻倍成長之表現。而
56%
39%

量測及自動化 半導體/矽光測試 Turnkey 其他

### Page 3

個股報告 
另外也預期 CPO 測試市場將在 2028 年成長至 3~7 億美元左右之規模。Advantest
則預期全球測試設備之 TAM 自 4 月預估的 110~120 億美元大幅上調至 130~145 億
美元。其中 SoC 測試機 TAM 達 105~115 億美元，記憶體測試機則約 25~30 億美
元。兩家測試設備龍頭皆對測試市場具有更正向之展望。而觀察終端晶片量而言 美系
GPU 大廠之晶片數量於 2027 年約在 600~700 萬顆之需求 ， 該公司之CPU 產品則約
500~600 萬顆需求 。 該部分需求之SLT 測試機台已於上半年陸續貢獻營收 。 下半年則
接續美系 A 客戶於 2027 年的需求，尤其其 CPU 需求量約達 500~600 萬顆，不亞於
GPU 客戶之晶片出貨量，帶動下半年半導體測試 SLT 機台出貨力道強勁。 
除此之外在 FT Handler 佈局方面為公司 2026 年成長動能之一 ， 過往即有該產品之製
造經驗，惟過去一段時間專注在 SLT Handler 開發，近期公司有望重回該領域，在整
體 FT Handler 仍處於供不應求的狀況下，有望貢獻一定份額之營收。在電源及半導
體產品線成長之態勢下，研究員預估 3Q26 營收 144.37 億元， QoQ+6.71%，
YoY+125.22%。EPS 11.47 元，QoQ-4.8%，YoY-3.74%。 
2H26 開始 CPO 相關測試機台加入 添增成長動能 
CPO 產品佈局方面，由於光引擎跟電子晶片需要被封裝在一起，在物理特性、對準精
度方面具有較大的挑戰。傳統之晶片測試僅需要透過探針接觸焊墊來讀取電子訊號，
但光訊號無法透過金屬探針做讀取，因此測試設備必須具備雷射光源與光譜儀，並透
過光纖來與晶片耦合才能檢測光學的損耗等。另外在精度方面也有所挑戰，原因為光
訊號之容錯率極低，只要位置偏差一微米，光學耗損就會劇增，因此無法用傳統的機
械對準來對位。為了解決以上問題，CPO 檢測設備同時要有測電及測光之能力，通常
在晶圓頂部與 ATE 對接，並透過探針卡進行電訊號之測試，而在晶圓底部對光纖陣列
進行光學檢測，此種同時進行光電測試的機台難度高，且額外多出了雷射光源、主動
對準系統等，單價將顯著高於傳統半導體用之 ATE 測試機台。目前公司在 CPO 進展
方面以取的多項成果及訂單 ， 其中在Insertion 3 光學引擎測試方面 ， 主要測試發射端
及接收端的功能性，例如測試發射端內部的電是否能成功驅動雷射光及測試光的功率
及訊號品質；或是測試光偵測器接收到光訊號後，其輸出的訊號是否達成客戶要求標
準等等，在該階段公司已取得大量訂單。由於 Insertion 3 在測量的時間較慢，使客戶
對機台需求量大幅增加，而競爭對手產能較低，使公司切入該市場且有機會拿到較大
的市場份額。 
而在 Insertion 4 部分有可分為 4-O 及 4-E 兩大部分 ，4-O 主要用來測試 CPO Switch
封裝後的光品質 ， 例如測量雷射的光功率 、 波長等 。 而4-E 則用來測量電訊號之品質 ，
例如 ASIC 晶片的電壓、電流等參數。目前公司也在該兩階段大有斬獲，成為未來重
要的成長動能。考量 2026~2027 年具多項成長動能，研究員預估 2026 年/2027 年
營收分別為 557.42 億元/754.73 億元，YoY+96.89% /35.4%。EPS 分別為 45.84 元
/63.26 元，YoY+66.24% /38.43%。 
未來兩年成長路徑明確 股價仍未高估 
電源測試機台於上半年已大幅成長，下半年則由半導體測試機台接棒， 3Q26~4Q26
則有 CPO 相關測試機台陸續貢獻營收 ，2H26~2027 年成長動能明確 。考量國際同業
如 Teradyne、Advantest 等測試領導廠商在同樣高成長性下享有超過 40X 之本益比 ，
研究員預期未來兩年為致茂之高成長期，應享有較高之評價，並預估 2027 年 EPS 約
63.26 元。考量以上，維持目標價 2,700 元(2027F PE 42x)，建議強力買進。

### Page 1

Yageo (2327.TW)
2Q26 Beat on Broad-Based Strength; Recalibrating TP as Sector 
Multiple Resets, Structural Thesis Intact
CITI'S TAKE 
Yageo’s 2Q26 results beat across scale and margins, with AI revenue mix 
rising to 16% and BB ratio climbing to 2.2, underscoring accelerating 
forward demand. Mgmt. ’s 3Q26E guidance for moderate rev growth and 
further margin improvement, alongside commodity/specialty UTR set to 
reach 90%+, points to a tightening S/D backdrop, while rising customer 
interest in multi-year LTAs across AI, computing, and memory sectors 
signals extending demand visibility. We raise our 2026E-28E EPS forecasts 
by 11%-13% and lift our ASP growth CAGR assumptions. That said, we 
adjust our TP to NT$1,450 (from NT$1,500), applying a 35x average 2027-
28E EPS (vs. 40x prior) to reflect the sector’s ongoing, deleveraging-driven 
multiple compression, while maintaining our positive view and Buy rating.
2Q26 results beat on scale and margins — Yageo’s reported 2Q26 net profit at 
NT$9.4bn (+18% QoQ/+88% YoY) beat Citi/cons forecasts by 15%/3%, driven by 
better scale, margin expansion, and opex control. GPM came in at 38.5% (+0.4ppt 
QoQ/+2.9ppts YoY), below cons estimate as higher commodity shipments diluted 
mix; however, we are starting to see early pricing pass-through flow into margins. AI 
revenue mix rose to 16% (from c.14-15% in 1Q26), while the BB ratio climbed MoM to 
2.2 by end-Jun (vs. 1.3 at AGM), pointing to robust forward demand visibility.
Positive from all leading indicators — Mgmt. guided 3Q26 revenue to moderate 
growth with GPM/OPM improving slightly, as commodity and specialty UTR are set 
to rise from 2Q26’s 80%/85% to 90%+/90%+, and mgmt. does not rule out further 
price hikes if raw material costs keep climbing. Mgmt. further flagged rising 
customer interest in signing LTAs across AI, computing, and memory end-markets, 
implying underlying demand is running ahead of prior expectations. We accordingly 
raise our 2025-28E ASP growth CAGR assumptions to 22%/23%/27% (from 
20%/16%/25%) across Yageo’s MLCC/resistor/tantalum lines.
Implications — We raise our 2026E-28E earnings forecasts by 11%-13% to reflect 
stronger results and guidance. We adjust our TP to NT$1,450, implying 35x avg. 
2027E-28E EPS (from NT$1,500 based on 40x), a moderate step-down reflecting 
the sector’s deleverage-driven multiple compression rather than any change to 
Yageo’s earnings trajectory. We expect profitability and growth visibility to keep 
improving through the cycle. Reiterate Buy.

### Page 8

Yageo (2327.TW)
30 July 2026 Citi Research
8
Yageo
Company description
Established in 1977 and listed in 1993, Yageo is a leading supplier of passive
electronic components based in Taiwan. The company offers a wide array of
products including MLCC, chip resistors, magnetics, tantalum, and other
components, aiming to be the total solution provider for its customers. By
capacity, the company currently ranks No. 1 in chip resistor and tantalum and
No. 3 in MLCC and inductor globally. Key customers of Yageo include leading

EMS, ODMs, and distributors. The company also has 29 sales offices, 51
manufacturing sites, and 20 R&D centers in 29 nations worldwide.
Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on Yageo shares. Yageo is widely known as a passive
electronic component supplier. Although passive components are generally
viewed as a commodity, Yageo is no longer a commodity play, in our view,
given the company’s foray into premium markets through M&As, which
should help mitigate the impact of sector cyclicality and order volatility. We

expect a more diversified product portfolio to drive stable earnings and better
margins, while improving utilization in its commodity component business
following the end of inventory digestions should add additional fuel.
Valuation
We set our target price at NT$1,450, based on 35x average 2027E-28E
earnings. We value Yageo based on PE as we believe the company’s earnings
performance and industry dynamics would be the key focuses of the market.
Our target multiple of 35x is at the higher end of the trading range since 2022.

During this period the company has gone through the cycles, successfully
integrated the acquired business to enhance its product offerings, and
started to deliver stable margins and earnings.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price and rating include: 1) slower-than-
expected demand recovery; 2) intensified peer and pricing competition; 3)
execution risks associated with M&A integration and synergies; and 4) FX and
interest rate fluctuations.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

Bull/Bear: Chipbond Technology (6147.TWO)

### Page 6

Chipbond Technology (6147.TWO)
31 July 2026 Citi Research
6
Chipbond Technology
Company description
Chipbond, established in 1997, provides turnkey services for back-end
assembly processing of LCD driver ICs, from wafer bumping to packaging.
Chipbond is the largest LCD driver IC back-end supplier.
Investment strategy
We rate Chipbond shares as Buy, as we see signs of stabilization in its main
DDIC business. Despite tepid smartphone demand, OLED would partially

support its ASP of its testing business with penetration on the rise steadily.
Meanwhile, the company would potentially gain share in DDIC from Korean
packaging peers as well. The company would also raise the price to customers
to reflect the inflation. Further, the company is developing optical products
with its gold bumping technology. Given its leading position in gold bumping,
we believe Chipbond would benefit from it meaningfully in the next few years.

Valuation
Our target price for Chipbond of NT$230 is derived from a DCF model. We use
a 1-year forward-looking DCF-based valuation (ie, end-2027) to reflect
Chipbond’s ability to generate stable cash. Using a risk-free rate of 4.3%, a
market risk premium of 7%, and an equity beta of 0.92, we calculate
Chipbond's WACC as 10.2%. Our model assumes 2% long-term revenue
growth starting in 2036E and EBIT margin of 18.6% in 2028E with GM
expansion in the following years. Our target price is equivalent to a P/E of

41x/28x 2026/27E and P/B of 3.4/3.2x 2026/27E.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) lower-than-expected photonics contribution; 2) lower-than-
expected smartphone sales, especially for OLED smartphone models; 3)
lower-than-expected PCNB demand or 4K TV penetration, and 4) failure to
raise price to at least pass through the inflation.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

Catalyst Watch on Hon Precision (7769.TW)
Direction: Upside
Duration: Within 90 Days (expires 27 Aug 2026)
Date Added: 28 May 2026
Catalyst: Thematic driven
We are constructive on Hon Precision's earnings growth outlook driven by its solid engagement in global leading AI
projects. For 2Q earnings, our estimates are higher than consensus as we expect higher margins from a better sales mix
and operating leverage, which we believe could drive strong >100% YoY earnings growth. Also, though a long-term

opportunity, we believe co-packaged optics (CPO) engagement with key customers could also drive significant upside
potential.

Company description
Hon Precision is a leading Taiwanese manufacturer specializing in IC
semiconductor automation equipment. Headquartered in Taichung City,
Taiwan, it is most known globally for providing high-precision IC test
handlers, temperature control solutions, and complete automated integrated
solutions for major OSAT and IDM customers.
Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on Hon Precision as we view it sitting in a structurally

protected position at the heart of the AI/HPC back-end test cycle, with
growth visibility extending well into the next few years — the company holds
>90% share of the AI/HPC FT handler market and 60-70% of the ASIC SLT
market. We forecast a solid strong sales revenue CAGR in 2025-28E with
gross margin continuing to expand. In our view, the binding constraint on Hon
Precision's near-term trajectory is now capacity, rather than demand —
placing the company in the early phase of a multi-year structural growth and

margin-expansion cycle.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$11,000 is based on a target PER multiple of 36x applied
to the average of our 2027E/28E EPS estimates. Our PER target of 35x is set
at 1-std above its 1-year PER average, justified by its high share of the AI/HPC
testing handler market, which we estimate could support an earnings CAGR
of 77% in 2025-28, higher than its 3-year CAGR at c40% in 2022-25. Our
estimated 77% earning CAGR is also higher than the +40% average

(Bloomberg consensus) of its global testing equipment peers. As its peers
have been trading at  average  36x PER on 2027 /28 consensus EPS in the past
1 year, we believe Hon Precision deserves a further re-rating to our target PER
of 36x.
Risks
Our quantitative risk rating system, which looks at historical share price
volatility, rates Hon Precision High Risk. However, we are not assigning a High
Risk rating as we forecast an earnings CAGR of > 75%, significantly stronger

than the growth outlook for its global peers. Also, we note its dominant
position in AI/HPC handler market and expanding market TAM opportunity,
which support our constructive view over its growth outlook.
Key downside risks that could impede the shares from reaching our target
price: 1) Global AI demand slowdown  driving weaker capacity build for
semiconductor equipment; 2) unexpected share losses at its major AI
projects; 3) slower-than-expected capacity ramp at its new facilities, or the

company taking a long time to find/secure new sites; 4) slower-than-
expected development of its CPO business; 5) shorter-than-expected testing
time leading to lower demand for its products; 6) weak adoption of high-

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

### Page 2

Realtek (2379.TW)
30 July 2026 Citi Research
2
Realtek
Valuation
Our target price for Realtek is set at NT$630 based on our target PE multiple of 17x to our 2026-2027 EPS average. We believe
our PER of 17x, in line with its 3-year forward PER average, is justified by its solid growth opportunity in automotive and
switch/PON business. We also believe its continued efforts in products mix optimization should help to support its margins
expansion. At our target price, the shares would trade at a 2026/27E P/B of 5x/4x.

Risks
Key downside risks to our target price include: 1) Slowdown in technology migration for its networking business; 2) Weaker-
than-expected growth momentum for automotive Ethernet sales; 3) Worse-than-expected PC/NB and consumer electronic
demand; 4) Slower-than-expected GM expansion due to pricing competition from peers; and 5) Higher-than-expected
memory cost burden to dampen the order outlook from its networking and PC/NB clients.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

Idea
2027 LEAP revenue guidance raised from US$5.5bn to at least US$7.5bn; we now
forecast US$8.5bn: On the July 30 earnings call, management said that 2026 LEAP
(leading-edge advanced packaging and testing) is tracking ahead and raised guidance
from US$3.5bn to US$3.7bn. Management also lifted the 2027 LEAP target from US
$5.5bn to at least US$7.5bn, a strong beat vs. both our estimate and the buy-side
bogey of ~US$6bn. We attribute this to strong AI-related demand, including strong

server CPU, which has shown meaningful development over the past quarter. We
now expect LEAP revenue of US$3.7bn in 2026 and US$8.5bn in 2027, mostly
driven by strong CPU (full process) and ASIC/GPU demand (oS). See Exhibit 4 for
our detailed LEAP revenue breakdown.
Another rise in 2026e capex; 2027e capex could remain high: In our prior report
ASE: Strong AI customer demand to trigger price hike, we noted that Taiwan's OSAT
capacity is full and that ASE and KYEC are devoting record-high capex to expand. As

expected, the company raised 2026e capex again from US$8.5bn to US$10.5bn. It is
simultaneously building 13 greenfield and seven brownfield sites. Management
indicated that 2027e capex could remain high, suggesting extremely strong demand
in the following years and limited concern about potential demand disruption from
Intel EMIB. This also echoes TSMC’s latest earnings call, in which it welcomed Intel’s
role in helping alleviate advanced packaging supply constraints.

Gross margin continues to expand: ASE’s ATM margin expansion is ahead of our
expectation. The company said it is likely to exceed the high end of its structural
range (30%) by 4Q26 and expects margins to keep rising sequentially in 2027. We
attribute the expansion to higher LEAP exposure, price hikes, and higher utilization
from legacy process as well.
Raise price target to NT$628; stay OW: We view the implied target P/E of 21x for
2027e as attractive given the strong >70% earnings CAGR from 2025–2028e.

We see even more upside from LEAP in 2027
ASE reported 2Q26 margin of 21.0%, up 1.0ppts Q/Q, in line with both our estimate and
consensus. The margin expansion stemmed mostly from higher utilization rate with
greater operating efficiency.   Looking into 3Q26, the company guided for revenue to be up
21-22% with gross margin to stay flat Q/Q given higher EMS business growth from
memory price hike. The company expects ATM margin to exceed the ceiling of the

structural range (30%) by 4Q26 and continues to expand sequentially in 2027.

We now forecast  ASE's LEAP revenue at US$3.7bn in 2026
and US$8.5bn in 2027
In our prior report, ASE Technology Holding Co. Ltd.: AI/CPO outlook exceeds our
aggressive assumptions; OW (30 Apr 2026), our bottom-up analysis suggested that  ASE's
LEAP (leading-edge advanced packing) revenue is likely to reach US$3.5bn in 2026 and US

$6bn in 2027, given strong demand from both oS (on substrate) outsourcing and CPU
CoWoS needs. However,  ASE management hinted that 2027 LEAP revenue could be more
than double new 2026e guidance of US$3.7bn, suggesting that 2027e LEAP revenue could
reach US$7.5bn or above. At the same time, we have also been seeing strong momentum
of AI-related demand over the past quarter, including but not limited to very strong server
CPU demand –  both Venice and Vera CPU could grow more than double in 2027, in our

view. We therefore update our LEAP revenue forecast below – our new assumptions are
US$3.7bn in 2026 and US$8.5bn in 2027.
Below are the assumptions in our bottom-up analysis, showing how we come up with
our US$8.5bn forecast.

### Page 4

M
Idea
4
The LEAP demand is mostly from oS  outsourcing from TSMC. While ASE remains the
major oS supplier to Nvidia GPU, our checks also indicate that the majority of Google TPU
and AMD MI400 series is likely to be outsourced to ASE given TSMC's clean room
limitations. The dollar content per chip for oS at ASE could range from US$100 to US
$200 in 2026, depending on the material cost, chip size, and margin of each product.  Our
current assumption of the oS outsourcing percentage from TSMC of major products is –

Nvidia almost 100%, TPU 70-90%, MI400 80%.
Meanwhile, we expect that AMD's Venice CPU could go for full CoWoS-like packaging at
ASE group (both ASE and SPIL). The initial allocation could be more to SPIL, given SPIL's
better production capability. The allocation could eventually reach 50:50 between SPIL
and ASE. By assuming US$8k per CoWoS wafer cost at ASE and that the chip per CoWoS
wafer could be around 25-26 chips for Venice CPU, we expect the dollar content per

Venice CPU to be slightly above US$300.
Besides the major products mentioned above, we also expect Vera CPU and other AI ASIC
chips (Microsoft Maia, Meta MTIA, etc.) to go through either oS or full CoWoS-like
process at ASE, while Vera could now show >2x volume in 2027, from our industry checks.

Valuation methodology
We lift  our price target from NT$558 to NT$628: This reflects our earnings estimate
revisions for 2026-28.
We continue to derive our price target using the base case value from a residual income
model. All of the key assumptions are unchanged, including cost of equity of 9.8% (beta
of 1.3, risk premium of 6% and risk-free rate of 2%),  intermediate growth rate of 9%, and
terminal growth rate of 3.0%.
Our bull case value rises from   NT$655 to NT$700, while our bear case  value increases

from  NT$355 to NT$380, implying 23x and 13x our 2027e EPS, respectively

NT$628.00
21x 2027e EPS
Strong cycle with AI/advanced packaging asthe main driving force: Demand for wire-bonding and ﬂip-chip from consumer techdemand is recovering while most of thecapacity in Taiwan is full in general. Theadvanced packaging business should be themajor source of margin accretion in thecoming years. Pricing for ATM businesses islikely to rise 5-20% in 2026. CoWoSoutsourcing from TSMC may aid its top lineand margin proﬁle as well as the CPOopportunity in 2027/2028.

NT$380.00
13x 2027e EPS
Semis enter down-cycle, with less back-endoutsourcing and big customers searching formore cost-effective alternatives: The macrosituation weakens, impairing semi demand.The need for second sources causes bigcustomers to shift back-end orders awayfrom ASE, along with pricing erosion givencompetition. SiP adoption remains limitedoutside of wearables. Gross margins keepdeteriorating given competition fromChinese peers.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

M
Update
AU Optronics  | Asia Pacific
New Applications for Glass to
Take Longer
We don't see any strong catalysts from here with a lukewarm
outlook for its core business and limited contributions from new
applications. Stay EW.
3Q26 guidance below seasonal: Management guided for display revenue (48% of
2Q26 mix) to decline modestly QoQ as consumer electronics demand should be
relatively  soft amid elevated component costs and earlier restocking in 1H26.

Mobility revenue (29% of 2Q26 mix) should remain roughly flat  QoQ as Europe
enters the slow season and China sees weakening demand. Vertical revenue (19% of
2Q26 mix) is expected to rise by low single digits % QoQ as orders from commercial
displays and green solutions remain stable. We view 3Q26 guidance as below
seasonal, although the revenue mix might turn a bit more favorable since the
mobility and vertical business generated  higher operating margins of 3.8% and 5.6%,

respectively, vs. display at -3.2% in 2Q26. For the longer term, it maintains its target
to have 70% of revenue from mobility and vertical solutions by 2030 (vs. 43% in
2025).
New business initiatives to  take time: For glass in advanced packaging, AUO has
been developing the TGV and RDL process for FOPLP and glass core. It is engaging
with potential customers for LEO antenna modules and micro LED optical modules,
which we think might see only limited revenue contribution in the coming years.

Similar to its display supply chain peers, it is looking into the glass core substrate
space and thinks it might take another 2-3 years for the process to mature. Mini/
micro LED is still on AUO's agenda for wearables and automobile applications.
though we think the complexity in its process remains a big hurdle for this ultimate
display technology to be commercialized in mainstream use cases. For CPO, it is in
the designing and sampling process with one optical interconnect supplier, although

we think it will take more time for AUO's integrated solution (micro LED +
photodiode + glass substrate) to be adopted as the industry is still trying to sort out
all of the issues in the current architecture.
More asset disposals: AUO announced plans to sell its Kaohsiung and Huaya fabs
for expected gains of NT$4.28bn and NT$13.39bn, respectively; timing for
recognition has not been finalized.
Stay EW: With a lackluster outlook for its core business and emerging businesses

2
Results Summary
Earnings turnaround in 2Q26: Off  revenue of NT$70,891mn (+3% QoQ/+2% YoY), gross
margin expanded by 1.2ppt to  13% on a more favorable product mix and improving
operating efficiency. With the opex ratio flat QoQ at 12.7%, operating profit was NT
$218mn, slightly better than MSe of a   NT$153mn loss but in line with consensus of profit
NT$238mn.
With a non-op gain of NT$2,533mn (mainly from asset disposal gain of ~NT$2.3bn), net

income came in at NT$1,343mn (EPS NT$0.18), surpassing  our expectation of NT$65mn
(EPS NT$0.01)  and consensus of NT$356mn (EPS NT$0.05).

### Page 3

M
Update
Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
AU Optronics (2409.TW)
Base case, 2026e P/B of 1.4x. We believe price/book value better reflects AUO's intrinsic value,
in view of the industry's relatively cyclical nature. The stock's mid-cycle average has been 0.8x
P/B, but it has re-rated due to its CPO and LEO businesses. We see our target multiple as
reasonable in consideration of the company's business diversification efforts, although they

will take time to contribute.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected end-demand
n Disciplined capacity control among peers
n Favorable panel pricing environment
n Faster progress on new business initiatives
Risks to Downside
n Macro headwinds leading to weak demand
n Faster yield improvement among Chinese players, leading to more supply
n Faster OLED panel adoption among large-size applications
n Slower progress on new business initiatives

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Document 118: 260731_daiwa_guc

Source PDF: all report pdf/260731_daiwa_guc.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-07-31
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: Global Unichip (GUC) reported 2Q26 results in line with our expectation mainly 
due to a higher-than-expected revenue yet offset by a lower gross margin on unfavourable 
product mix. Management sounded constructive for 2H26 due to improved support from 
TSMC’s wafer capacity allocation. We continue to see GUC as well positioned to capture 
the AI-driven ASIC opportunities, while increasing exposure to automotive program offers 
an additional growth lever. We rate the stock Hold (3) and will be reviewing our model in 
due course.

Highlight 
• 2Q26 results and 2H26 outlook. GUC reported 2Q26 net profit of c.TWD1.6bn, 
1%/13% above our/consensus estimates. Revenue beat our estimate (see our memo) 
thanks to a faster AI CPU ramp on better capacity support from TSMC, and some NRE 
projects moving into the late stage of their ship-out cycle ahead of schedule. Gross 
margin (GM), however, came in at 21.5%, 3.6pp below our estimate due to a less 
favourable product mix, as turnkey revenue and late-stage NRE project ship-out 
typically carry lower GM. Opex was well managed at 9.4% of revenue despite higher 
R&D expense. GUC looks well positioned for a robust growth in 2H26, as TSMC wafer 
capacity allocation has turned out even more positive than in 1H26, supporting our 
current forecasts for 2H26 revenue to grow 22% comparing to that in 1H26. 
Management indicated the 2H26 product mix will remain broadly similar to that of 2Q26, 
with cloud segment revenue continuing to account for around 80% of total, while crypto 
business gradually subsides and automotive revenue increases.

• Well positioned in the AI cycle. Strategically, GUC should continue to benefit from its 
competitiveness in advanced packaging (AP) and its close relationship with TSMC, 
which allows it to leverage its AP edge to win new customers and move up the value 
chain to capture more value-added design content. Management expressed confidence 
in its capability of securing more projects from major cloud service providers (CSP), and 
said that it can support alternative AP technologies upon request by customers for 
customisation. All told, we continue to see GUC as a beneficiary of the AI megatrend, 
with AI CPU/accelerator and automotive ASIC businesses supporting its growth to back 
our key thesis centring on its integrated business model of “ADS + APT” (advanced 
design services, advanced packaging technologies). We will be reviewing our model in 
due course.

Recommendation 
We rate GUC Hold (3) with a 12M TP of TWD4,525, based on our 4-quarter forward PER 
target of 75x. The stock has corrected more than 30% off its recent peak in May, improving 
its risk/reward profile on valuation grounds, in our opinion. Key upside/downside risk to our 
current rating would be any more capacity released by its key foundry partner/weaker-
than-expected margin execution.

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Following our results memo, we lift our forecasts and TP for 
UMC to factor in its stronger-than-expected wafer shipment on AI and 
automotive & industrial (A&I) strength, followed by order reallocation as a 
result of the foundry capacity consolidation. We expect its price hike to go 
through for extra margin accretion and earnings growth, premised upon its 
optimal utilisation. That said, we remain comfortable with our Hold (3) call 
on the stock as we believe the volume and ASP strength is already in the 
price, given the equity market’s hefty expectation over 2027-28. TSMC 
(2330 TT, TWD2,205, Buy [1]) is our preferred foundry exposure.

What’s the impact: Forecast changes. We raise our 2027-28E EPS by 
10-16% for UMC on 2 reasons: stronger-than-expected wafer shipment and 
price hike to go through effective in 2027. Thanks to strong demand for AI 
companion chips such as power management, FPGA (field-programmable 
gate array) and connectivity including new ramp of SiPh (silicon-photonics) 
based on 12” wafers for competitiveness, as well as robust demand for A&I 
chips more than enough to offset consumer weakness, we expect UMC’s 
capacity utilisation rate (CUR) to reach 91% in 3Q26. We further forecast 
the CUR to reach 100% in 2H27, thanks to extra demand from customers’ 
order reallocation as a result of the foundry capacity consolidation, 
including PSMC’s (6770 TT, not rated) fab P5 sale and TSMC’s mature 
capacity optimisation. Of note, we hike our 2026E EPS by 60% which is 
inflated by UMC’s hefty non-operating gains of TWD30bn in 2Q26 from 
financial assets marked-to-market and investment gains.

Price hike still likely. Management expects some uplift in wafer prices for 
2027; we concur since given our projected CUR ramping trajectory, we still 
see a good chance for the price hike to go through, so long as the AI 
buildout cycle sustains to drive favourable supply/demand dynamics amid 
the foundry capacity consolidation. We are modelling 2-5% hikes effective 
from 1Q27 across selected process nodes especially related to the AI and 
A&I applications, which should continue to drive UMC’s 28nm-and-below 
ramp to 45% of revenue (37% in 2Q26) for blended ASP improvement.

What we recommend: Given our forecast revisions, we lift our 12M TP for 
UMC to TWD108 (previous: TWD71), based on our higher ROE-adjusted 
PBR target of 3.3x (previous: 2.4x) on 4-quarter-forward BVPS due to 
higher ROE trajectory. Yet, we keep our Hold (3) rating on the stock as we 
believe its volume and ASP story already in the price. Key upside/downside 
risks would be any price hikes above our expectation/geopolitical 
overhangs (war, FX, interest rate) weighing on end-demand.

How we differ: Our 2027-28E EPS are 14-19% below the consensus 
forecasts as we believe the street is too bullish on UMC’s pricing power.

31 July 2026

Price hike to go through? Still likely

➢ Increasing forecasts on favourable wafer loading and pricing 
➢ Price hike likely to go through on improving supply/demand  
➢ Maintaining Hold (3) on valuation grounds; higher 12M TP of TWD108

Company profile 
Incorporated in Taiwan, United Microelectronics Corp (UMC) is one of the leading semiconductor 
foundries in the world and second largest in Taiwan. UMC offers a series of foundry services from 
photo-mask, wafer fabrication to wafer probe & test. Its wafer fabs are spread over Taiwan, China and 
Singapore, with technology offerings ranging from 0.25u and above, 0.18/0.15u, 0.13u, 90nm, 65nm, 
45/40nm, 28/22nm to 14nm FF.

### Page 1

MediaTek

Cloud ASIC ramp approaching a pivotal 
phase at compelling valuation

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 5,230 TWD | Price: 3,555 TWD

3Q26 sales and margins holding up on resilient smart edge 
2Q26 sales was -1% QoQ, on strong smart edge +19% and power IC +14% balanced by 
continued weak smartphone demand. Despite better mix, rising component cost and 
similar scale kept GM/OpM stable QoQ at 46.2%/15.0%, taking 2Q26 EPS to NT$15.28. 
3Q26 sales was guided +0-5% QoQ led by strong connectivity and auto projects ramp 
while smartphone remains muted with new flagship ramp dragged by broader market 
softness and its mix optimization. 3Q26 GM/OpM is guided in line at 46%/15% midpoint 
and we expect it to reach upper half on better mix and its effort to pass through cost. 
’27 ASIC TAM & share lifted; further diversification in ‘28 
In contrast to market concern on TPU design and production delay, Mediatek raised its 
data center ASIC revenue guide to US$2bn+ in ’26 and US$12-16bn in ’27 based on its 
upward revision of AI accelerator ex-HBM TAM to US$80bn (vs. US$75-80bn prior) as it 
sees stronger demand and TSMC’s capacity support. Notably, it highlighted the design 
of TPUv9t is on track for ramp in early ’28 and it is working with several customers on 
data center ASICs. Backed by its advanced technology portfolio including 448G SerDes, 
CPC (co-packaged copper) and 3.5D packaging and design flows suitable for large reticle 
size ASIC design, we believe Mediatek has started co-developing the AI accelerator ASIC 
with xAI set for tape out in 4Q27 along with optionality of other projects (e.g. Meta and 
Toyota/Denso ADAS ASICs), lifting its cloud ASIC business to US$45.8bn in 2028E. 
Troughing '27-28 valuation provides attractive opportunity 
With near term business and ASIC progress largely in line with our preview, we keep our 
‘26/27/28 EPS at NT$68.61/NT$137.76/NT$347.25. Reiterate our Buy rating and PO at 
NT$5,230 on 23x 2H27-1H28E P/E. Following the recent pullback, the share is attractive 
at 25x/10x '27/'28E P/E (vs. its P/E range of 8-25x), backed by its solid sales/earnings 
outlook in '26-28E at 85%/130% CAGR as operating leverage could accelerate backed by 
cloud/auto ASIC projects ramp and potential in edge AI with strong smart edge solutions.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
MediaTek is a fabless IC design company supplying to 
wireless communications and digital consumer markets. 
Before taking leadership in mobile SoC area, it had built 
solid ground in the TV SoC and optical disk drive markets. 
The firm invests heavily in R&D to seek better positioning 
in the 5G cycle, and has a complete offering including ASIC, 
Wi-Fi, PMIC etc. to address applications beyond 
smartphone, such as IoT and auto.

Investment Rationale 
We have Buy rating on MediaTek as we think MediaTek is 
ready and has the strength to brace for the upcoming 
proliferation of IoT devices and opportunities in growing 
markets like ASIC, ARM-based PC, auto, and etc., and we 
believe its solid balance sheet and cashflow could provide 
downside support to the share price. Meanwhile, we expect 
5G smartphone penetration to be a multi-year theme for 
the company, with its better competitive landscape in 
duopoly 5G era and an improved margin profile.

### Page 4

Price objective basis & risk 
MediaTek (MDTKF) 
Our PO of NT$5,230 is based on 23x 2H27-1H28 P/E. as we are constructive on its 
broadening ASIC pipeline to drive earnings upgrade cycle and re-rating as it locks in 
more supply.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) better than expected smartphone demand, especially in 
China, (2) a better pricing in mobile communication segment thanks to alleviated 
competition and rising exposure to high-end/flagship market, and (3) an even more 
superior investment return on non-op side to uplift its net cash position. (4) Unexpected 
collaboration with global technology leaders leads to higher than expected 
margins/profits

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) weaker-than-expected end-market situation in 
consumer-related smartphone/TV space, (2) macro challenges including inflation/policy-
related pressures, (3) rising competition from existing players and/or newcomers in 
smartphone/TV SoC (system on chip) space, and (4) ASP/margin pressure caused by each 
or all of the above and/or rising foundry costs. (4) Bidding process for Meta Inference 
ASIC is slower than expected.

### Page 1

M
Update
Chroma Ate Inc.  | Asia Pacific
Our Thoughts on the Share
Price Movement
Chroma's share price was down 6.7% (limit-down during the session) vs.  the
TAIEX up ~1%.
We think the underperformance was mainly driven by two investor concerns:
• Rubin Ultra might only have a TDP of  1,800W as the mainstream spec, which
might affect its GPU SL T growth in 2027.
• Chroma might not win CPU SL T orders from one major chip vendor.
Our views:
• We believe that 1,800W TDP for the Rubin Ultra GPU does not sound

reasonable. The TDP for the Rubin GPU already reaches 1,800-2,300W. Our
checks suggest that the mainstream spec for Rubin Ultra should be higher
than that of Rubin and will need a higher spec of SL T from Chroma as well.
• For the SL T business, Chroma used to focus more on the GPU market, but it
is turning  more aggressive on  CPUs  as well. It has already secured the CPU
SL T project with one hyperscaler and is qualifying with a major chip vendor

for its new CPU model.
• We stay OW on Chroma (price target NT$2,800, 45x 2027e P/E) and would
suggest building positions into the dip for the growth potential driven by
power, semiconductor, and photonics testing tools.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Chroma Ate Inc. (2360.TW)
Base case, 45x 2027e P/E. We use P/E as our valuation methodology because we think it better
captures Chroma's earnings growth momentum. The stock traded at an average valuation of
~19x P/E during 2014-25 with an operating profit CAGR of 20%, implying a PEG of 1.0. We think
our target multiple of 45x is justified by the 61% operating profit CAGR we project for

2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected macro conditions
n Stronger semi and photonics and power testing demand
n Margin improvement via better product mix
n Better opex control
Risks to Downside
n Macro slowdown
n Softer-than-expected AI capex
n Slower progress in the advanced packaging capacity expansion
n Weaker consumer electronics and automobile related demand
n Margin contraction arising from lack of revenue scale and increasing opex

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

3023.TT 信邦 
 用 MAGIC 產業創造更多 MAGIC！

[財務資訊更新]   
   公司簡介 
信邦成立於 1989 年，總部設立於新北市，為精密連接線束與相關組件大廠，透過醫
療器材、車用航太、綠能環境、工業應用與通訊等五大產業的布局，為全球客戶提供
高附加價值的連接線組服務。近期在機器人、半導體設備與低軌衛星 等也有所著墨。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 365 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】區 間 操 作 ，362 
【大盤指數/股價】43120 / 296

➢ 2Q26 工業 、 半導體與機器人等領域小量出貨 ， 產品組合持續優化 
信邦 2Q26 營收達 90.19 億元，QoQ+8.8%、YoY+16.6%，主因綠能、
工業需求回升 ， 且半導體 、 機器人與無人機開始小量出貨 ； 毛利率24.5%
持平上季，雖受低毛利的伺服器機櫃及汽車產品出貨增加衝擊，但高毛
利的客製化工業應用占比提升，抵銷了部分跌幅；營業利益率 10.4%， 
-1.1pts. QoQ，-0.9pts. YoY，主要受新研發、調薪與運費導致費用率上
升影響；業外因匯兌與金融評價利益增加，助攻獲利，2Q26 稅後 EPS 
3.76 元，YoY+19%；累計 1H26 營收 173.11 億元，YoY+8.9%，稅後
EPS 7.37 元，YoY+5%。 
➢ MAGIC 五大應用中，工業占比最大、汽車成長性最高 
信邦在工業應用因人形機器人、智慧交通、半導體設備與AI 基礎建設等
實體 AI 四大核心領域開始迎來收割期，2026 年與 2027 年占比將擴大
至 31.7%與 34%。其中，人形機器人已打入5 家美企，單台線束價值逾
1,500 美元，並結盟正淩與宇隆，預計2026 年及 2027 年出貨 1 萬與 2
萬套 ， 台中新廠2H27 量產後更將加速成長 。 汽車領域則具最高成長性 ，
預估 2026 年與 2027 年增幅達 41.4%與 23.3%。公司策略調整有成：
中國轉向服務 比亞迪(002594.CH)等在地一線新能源車廠；日本市場靠
液冷快充直銷日系大廠；美歐市場則受惠電動車發展，除完成 NACS 充
電線束量產準備，亦積極參展推廣歐規輕量化充電產品。 
➢ 2026~2027 年多項題材陸續發酵 ，調升至買進建議， 目標價365 元 
投研部預估信邦 2026 年/2027 全年營收分別為 361.51 億元/415.97 億
元，YoY+16.5% /15.1%；公司在人形機器人、AI 伺服器機櫃專用的大
電流線束 、 半導體設備與低軌衛星通訊用線束等高毛利產品將陸續出貨 ，
預估毛利率將上升至 24.6%/24.8%；隨著高毛利產品量產後，初期較高
的研發費用將逐漸被抵銷，預估稅後 EPS 分別為 15.19 元/18.16 元。在
評價方面 ， 目前PER 為 19.5X / 16.3X， 過去兩年PER 區間在 14X~26X，
考量公司在台灣與海外所布局的 MAGIC 五大高階應用領域深具潛力，
其中長期 TAM(總潛在市場)仍將處於高速成長階段，信邦將逐步在多地
擴大滲透率，評價有提升的空間，因此調升至買進評等，目標價 365 元
(2027F PER 20X)。

個股報告

公司簡介 
信邦成立於 1989 年 ， 總部設立於台灣新北市汐止區 ， 為高階連接線束與精密連接器組
件的領導大廠 ， 目前已由早期的低毛利消費性電子代工廠 ， 成功轉型為高客製化與高附
加價值的系統整合方案提供商 。 公司為避免單一產業的景氣循環波動 ， 以共同設計與製
造的方式跨足統稱為 「MAGIC」 的五大高階應用領域：1) M (Medical，醫療器材)：高
精密醫療級連接線束 ，應用於斷層掃描 、 超音波與心導管設備等高門檻領域 。醫療線束
對訊號傳輸的零失真與抗干擾(EMI)要求極高，且必需通過生物相容性檢驗，信邦旗下
產品具備耐高溫高壓滅菌與耐重複彎折數十萬次等特性，供應國際一線醫療設備大廠
如 GE 與 Philips 等；2) A (Automotive，車用與航太)：電動車用高壓動力線束、感測
系統、以及全球首張通過 UL 安全認證的 DC 直流快充液冷系統(HPC)。車用線束需長
期暴露於高低溫差 、 劇烈震動與高電壓的環境中 ， 信邦的液冷快充槍透過在線纜內注入
冷卻液，成功在縮小線徑、減輕重量的同時，實現超高功率充電而不發燙，切入歐美地
區的充電基礎設施市場；3) G (Green，綠能環境)：風力發電大型控制櫃線束、太陽能
逆變器連接線等綠色能源基礎設施 。 綠能產品面臨極端氣候的考驗 ， 如離岸風電的鹽霧
腐蝕及陸域風電的極地低溫 ， 信邦除了供應相關線材 ， 也具備大型控制櫃的組裝與測試
能力，為全球風電巨頭如 Vestas (VWS.CO)的長期戰略夥伴，儘管綠能產業近年受到
美國政策波動影響，但在「2050 淨零碳排」的框架下，全球對綠能基礎建設線材依賴
度依然不減；4) I (Industrial，工業應用)、工業自動化機械手臂關節線、半導體整機系
統模組組裝 、 人形機器人全身關鍵線束 、 半導體高階微影與蝕刻設備無塵室線束 。該領
域為信邦近年成長動能最強勁的板塊 ， 在人形機器人應用場景中 ， 機器人全身擁有數十
個靈活的動力關節 ， 需承受極高頻率的扭轉與拉伸， 且空間極度壓縮 ，信邦目前已成功
打入國際人形機器人新創企業供應鏈， 並取得唯一供應商資格 。而在半導體領域 ，公司
也已從零件供應商升級為設備整機組裝企業，已成為國際科技大廠的合作夥伴； 5) C 
(Communication，通訊週邊)：物聯網裝置、雲端資料中心高速微細同軸線、AI 伺服
器液冷散熱模組線組、低軌衛星的地面接收站線材、5G 微基站戶外防水連接線。隨著
AI 伺服器功耗暴增，傳統風扇散熱難以負荷，信邦開發了用於測試與連接散熱器性能
的液冷線組，其 4U 浸沒式微型液冷運算模組與 Dow 進行深度戰略合作。公司同時也
透過旗下子公司睿信電子在太空經濟中也參與了低軌衛星地面接收裝置的高頻連接線
材研發，為 SpaceX(星鏈計畫 Starlink)的供應商。 
圖 1：信邦五大高階應用領域

資料來源：信邦法說會簡報

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
2Q26 工業、半導體與機器人等領域小量出貨，產品組合持續優化 
信邦 2Q26 營收 90.19 億元，QoQ+8.8%，YoY+16.6%，主因為綠能需求增溫、高附
加價值的工業應用需求持續增溫 ， 再加上半導體設備 、 人形機器人與無人機等領域開始
小量出貨所致。觀察營收細項，綠色能源佔 18.6%、工業應用佔 35.6%、醫療照護佔
9.7%、汽車佔 16.6%、通訊佔 19.5%；毛利率 24.5%，持平於上季，YoY-0.2pts.，受
伺服器機櫃組裝業務比重提升影響，而該業務材料成本占比較高、附加價值相對較低，
加上汽車事業低毛利產品出貨比重增加 ， 對整體毛利率形成壓力 ， 然而工業應用產品線
具備高度客製化及較高的認證門檻 ， 擁有較佳的獲利能力 ， 隨著其營收占比提升 ，部分
抵銷了前述不利因素，使 2Q26 毛利率仍得以維持與 1Q26 相當的水準；營業利益率
10.4%，-1.1pts. QoQ，-0.9pts. YoY，衰退的原因來自公司在機櫃組裝投入較多新產
品研發費用 ， 同時還調整薪資 、 拓展市場以及支付相關運費 ， 導致費用率YoY+0.7pts.；
在營業外損益部分 ， 因匯兌損失減少及金融資產評價利益增加 ，2Q26 合併營業外利益
較去年同期增加 4,743 萬元，成為獲利成長的來源；整體而言，公司在 2Q26 的產品
組合持續優化，稅後 EPS 3.76 元，QoQ+4.4%，YoY+19%。累計 1H26 營收 173.11
億元，YoY+8.9%；累計 1H26 稅後 EPS 7.37 元，YoY+5%。 
圖 2：信邦營收組合

資料來源：信邦年報、國票投顧整理 
MAGIC 五大應用中，工業占比最大、汽車成長性最高 
信邦 MAGIC 五大應用中，2025 年在醫療照護的營收佔比為 8.8%、汽車占比 16.1%、
綠色能源占比 20.3%、工業應用 30.6%、通訊 24.2%。 
展望未來， 投研部預估信邦在 2026 年與 2027 年占比仍然最大 ， 分別達31.7%與 34%，
主因為公司在實體 AI 的四大核心應用即將四件齊發，包含 1)人形機器人：因配線涵蓋
從頭到腳的範圍，單台機器人線束價值超過 1,500 美元，高於消費性電子與部分車款
電動車的單量線束價值， 且毛利率預估介於30%~35%， 為公司下一波的成長動能， 目
前已打入 5 家美系機器人公司，其中甚至成為 Figure 關鍵線束的唯一供應商。為了將
機器人線束與內部機電整合 ， 公司除了結盟轉投資的連接器公司正淩(8147)之外 ， 也與
高階精密金屬加工廠宇隆(2233)簽署合作備忘錄，由信邦負責高靈敏度線束與連接器、
電控模組以及 PCB 板，而宇隆負責 RV 減速機、行星減速機與諧波減速機。目前公司
已邁入少量至中量試產階段 ， 待2H27 台中梧棲新廠完工後加入量產 ， 總體而言 ，2026
年與 2027 年人形機器人線束出貨量預估各為 1 萬與 2 萬套，挹注總營收各為 5 億元
與 10 億元左右，目標於 2028 年挑戰 20 萬套；2)智慧交通與倉儲物流：信邦既有的

### Page 4

- 4 -

個股報告 
工業控制將拓展至多種場景的無人化營運 ， 包含與歐美大廠合作製造自動導引車(AGV)
與自主移動機器人(AMR)專用的高階線材 ， 另外信邦憑藉高階客製化能力也成功將技術
切入商用運輸無人機與電動垂直起降飛行器(eVTOL，俗稱空中計程車)供應鏈，提供具
備航太級安全認證的超輕量、強效抗干擾連接器模組；3)半導體設備：信邦相關產品已
從單純的線材零件延伸至高階機櫃整機組裝，旗下半導體設備客戶從過去的 2 家擴展
至目前的 6 家 ， 包含艾司摩爾(ASML.US)曝光機的超高傳輸線束等 ；4)電源與冷卻等 AI
基礎建設 ： 信邦與全球電力設備模組廠合作 ， 使CSP 大廠在集中式配線櫃(Power Rack)
與匯流排(Busbar Connector Harness) 中採用信邦的超大電流耐高壓主幹線束，另外
還積極切入資料中心中 AI 伺服器與機房的浸沒式液冷連接方案。 
投研部也預估信邦的汽車領域成長性最高， 在 2026 年與 2027 年分別為 41.4%與
23.3%，儘管公司信邦的中國車用產線減少了美系出口外銷，但全面轉向服務比亞迪等
體系的中國在地一線新能源品牌車廠， 專攻中國車廠外銷車款所需的高壓充電 、 電池管
理系統(BMS)及 ADAS 智慧駕駛感測模組線束 ； 公司在日本市場也頗有斬獲 ，自主研發
的直流快充液冷系統順利取得 UL 認證後成功打入日本大型車企 ，同時也透過台灣和中
國產線擴大對日系車廠車載影音與動力控制模組線束的直銷渗透與高度彈性支援；美
國與歐洲市場則受惠於電動車的發展而獲得訂單，信邦已完成對美國客戶 NACS(北美
充電標準)的連接器及高壓充電線束的量產準備，在 2026 年隨著當地基礎設施建設大
量出貨，而歐洲則採取積極的進攻策略，2026 年 6 月首次將電動車充電全系列產品帶
入歐洲 ， 參展慕尼黑Power2Drive Europe 2026 展會 ， 展出專為歐洲開發的歐規Type 
2 交流、CCS2 直流以及 CCS2 液冷充電線與充電插座，升級輕量化的全新設計。 
醫療照護與綠能估成長 17.3%與 11%，通訊產業則小幅衰退 0.9% 
此外，在醫療照護方面，過去佔整體交貨量約 50%的中國產線已經隨美系客戶要求而
移轉至美墨 ， 在俄亥俄州擴建規模達1.5 倍的新廠以及在墨西哥聖路易斯波托西新廠進
行美墨雙廠協作 ， 客製化藥物霧化器與醫用高壓電纜則持續獲歐系客戶積極採購 ， 再加
上供應給日本的高階客製化線束與診斷影像設備相關市佔逐漸增加， 因此預估營收
YoY+17.3%；在綠色能源方面，中國產線的綠能外銷比重在 2026 年因競爭激烈而持
續下降 ， 但日本目前存在小型綠能儲能設備需求 ， 信邦主要配合當地的能源系統整合商
供應高階儲能櫃與分散式微電網的工業級客製化連接線組，在美國墨西哥新廠與美國
俄亥俄州新廠已於近年完工，並在 2026 年步入產能全面放量期，另外公司也受惠於歐
洲風電 大廠 精簡 成本而 在 2026 年獲 得 與 Vestas (VWS.CO)、Siemens Gamesa 
(SGREN)等深化策略結盟的機會，因此預估營收 YoY+11%；在通訊領域方面，信邦正
積極爭取美國企業的低軌衛星地面接收站與陣列天線線束訂單，並針對美國大客戶提
供 AI 資料中心高頻高速傳輸線，惟 2026 年仍處於新舊產品交替期，需待 2027 年營
收才會顯著放大，歐洲通訊訂單主要集中於大型網通設備商的工業級交換機與骨幹網
路連接元件， 因此預估營收 YoY-0.9%。投研部預估 信邦 3Q26 營收 94.98 億元，
QoQ+5.3%，YoY+25.8%；毛利率 24.7%，+0.2pts. QoQ，+1.7pts. YoY；營業利益
率 10.7%，+0.3pts. QoQ，+2.4pts. YoY；稅後淨利率 10%，-0.1pts. QoQ，+0.4pts. 
YoY；稅後 EPS 3.96 元，QoQ+5.3%，YoY+30%。 
2026~2027 年多項題材陸續發酵，調升至買進建議，目標價 365 元 
以年度的角度來看，投研部預估信邦 2026 年/2027 全年營收為 361.51 億元/415.97
億元，YoY+16.5% /15.1%；公司在人形機器人、AI 伺服器機櫃專用的大電流線束、半
導體設備與低軌衛星通訊用線束等高毛利產品將陸續出貨， 預估毛利率將上升至 24.6% 
/24.8%；隨著高毛利產品量產後，初期較高的研發費用將逐漸被抵銷， 營業利益率預

營收走勢圖

研究員/莊佳蓉 EVA8894122@uni-psg.com

研究部維持順達買進投資建議，主係 : (1) 2Q26 雖受某一 BBU 機種
拉貨停滯影響，不如預期，惟7 月起拉貨將恢復正常，3Q26 營運及獲
利皆會顯著回升，最壞狀況已過；(2) 26 及 27 年持續擴產，較25 年
提升 3 倍，且稼動率從26 年 60-70%，提升至27 年 80%以上，客戶需
求維持強勁；(3)4Q26 將新增一 CSP 客戶，高壓專案涵蓋三家 CSP 客
戶。(4)受惠資料中心往 HVDC 發展趨勢不變，目前股價跌回 15 倍，
價值浮現 。綜上所述 ， 研究部微幅上修 27 年營收至 214 億元(原估 213
億元)、EPS 上修至 21.97 元(原估 20.42 元)，給予 22 倍本益比，目
標價上修至 485 元。

重點摘要： 
1. 2Q營運、EPS不如預期，但最壞已過 
2Q26營收34.91億元(原估36.46億元)，季增4.4%，年增7.4%，
營收達成率僅95%，低於預期，主係BBU某機種拉貨停滯。毛利
率13.8%(原估19.7%)，季減6.2pts，低於預期，主因Non-IT占
比僅約25~30% (原估佔比應達45~50%， 產品組合轉差，約當影
響5.2pts)。營益率6.5%(原估12.8%)，季減6.0pts，亦低於預
期。故EPS 1.34元(原估2.86元)，低於預期。

2. BBU拉貨7月恢復正常，3Q26營運及獲利將顯著改善 
展望3Q26，公司表示7月起拉貨已恢復正常，營運有望回復。
供應鏈調查，低壓產品因客戶議價能力強，砍價情形發生，故
研究部微幅 3Q26營收至41.18億元(原估43.27億元)，季增
18.0%，年增13.7%。毛利率因受到零組件漲價壓力，微幅下修
至20.7%(原估21.2%)，但季增6.9pts， 主係產品組合大幅轉佳
(預估Non IT比重達6成以上)；營益率13.9%(原估14.4%)，季增
7.4pts，EPS微幅下修至3.40元(原估3.72元)。整體而言 ，3Q26
營運將大幅好轉，OPM、GM也明顯改善。

3. 下半年新增一CSP客戶，27年進入高壓BBU量產期 
在BBU規格部分 ，1H26仍以3KW/5.5KW為主力 ，8KW/12KW預計於
2H26開始出貨。HVDC高壓BBU在26年持續送樣，預期在27年開
始量產，BBU規格持續提升。隨著新專案開出，產品將重新報
價，研究部預估GM將達30%以上。此外，公司亦表示，4Q26將
新增一家CSP客戶加入出貨，未來高壓專案涵蓋三家CSP。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

在產能部分，26年產能較25年擴增2倍，27年台灣、泰國廠仍
將持續擴充，稼動率可望由26年60~70%提升至27年80%以上，
整體BBU滲透率持續增加，公司Non-IT占比持續提升。

4. 營運谷底已過，目前評價僅15倍，投資價值浮現 
綜上所述 ，因2Q26 BBU拉貨停滯 ， 研究部下修2026年營收至152
億元(原估162億元)，年增15.1%；EPS下修至10.64元(原估
12.95元)。展望2027年，HVDC高壓BBU開始出貨，ASP同步墊高 ，
研究部微幅上修2027年營收至214億元(原估213億元)，年增
40.6%；EPS上修至21.97元(原估20.42元)。目前評價於低檔15
倍，考量營運最壞狀況已過，2H26營運及獲利將顯著改善，給
予 22 倍 本 益 比 ， 目 標 價 由 420 元 上 修 至 485 元 
(2027EPS*22PER)。

大客戶專案放量，Turnkey大成長 
投資評等 目標價 
買進 $6000 
前次投資建議 2026.2.2： 
「逢低買進」 ，目標價：$3100

報告日期：2026.8.3 研 究 員：林秀泓

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ CSP專案放量，營收動能延續到 2027 年。其他專案 2027 年貢獻。 
➢ CSP下一代 CPU 專案 2026 年底~2027 年初 tape out。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/7/31 收盤價： 3,805.00 ➢ 26Q2 營收 139 億元，QoQ+21.4%，YoY+127.6%，優
於預期。其中 NRE營收佔比 17%，Turnkey 佔比 83%。
NRE 營收 QoQ+40.6%，YoY+19.6%；Turnkey 營收
QoQ+18.2%，YoY+187.9%。毛利率21.6%(QoQ-5.6pt，
YoY-11.7pt)，因為產品組合變化，EPS 11.61元。 
➢ 我們估計 CSP專案放量使 Turnkey 營收持續強勁，預估
26Q3 營收 156 億元，QoQ+12.0%，YoY+80.7%。毛利
率 因 為 產 品 組 合 的 緣 故 ， 預 期 小 幅 下 降 到
20.1%(QoQ-1.5pt)。EPS 11.88元。 
➢ 2026 年最大貢獻為 CSP 的 CPU 專案 Turnkey，下一代
CPU 專案預計 2026 年底~2027 年初 tape out。車用方
面，26H2 中國客戶車用專案開始貢獻，預期車用營收補
上 Crypto 營收缺口。北美客戶目前專案進度持續，下一
代車用專案高機率取得。 
➢ 2026 年主要成長動能為 CSP 的 CPU 量產，並延續到
2027年，2027 年其他專案也開始貢獻營收。我們調整預
估 2026/2027 年營收 599 億元 /1,178 億元，
YoY+75.5%/+96.7%，EPS 50.86 元/95.65 元。 
➢ 目前股價處 74X 26(F)PER / 40X 27(F)PER，在近五年
PE 15~120X的區間中位。考量2026、2027 為大成長年，
投資評等上調到「買進」 ，上調6 個月目標價為 6,000 元
(63X 27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 58%。

年底~2027 年初 tape out。

戰略意義在於將先進技術如先進封裝在這些專案上進行試驗，成功後推廣給
更大型客戶。 
車用方面，26H2中國客戶車用專案開始貢獻，預期車用營收補上 Crypto 營
收缺口。北美客戶目前專案進度持續，下一代專案高機率取得。 
新領域佈局方面，CPO有所佈局，2026 年投入大量 RD資源在高速光通訊、

2026 年 CPU專案量產，2027年 Turnkey雙主線 
2026 年主要成長動能為 CSP 的 CPU 量產，並延續到 2027 年，2027 年其
他專案也開始貢獻營收。我們調整預估 2026/2027 年營收 599 億元/1,178 億
元，YoY+75.5%/+96.7%，淨利 68 億元/128 億元，YoY+80.8%/+88.1%，
EPS 50.86 元/95.65 元。 
目前股價處 74X 26(F)PER / 40X 27(F)PER，在近五年 PE 15~120X的區間
中位。考量 2026、2027為大成長年，投資評等上調到「買進」，上調 6個月
目標價為 6,000元(63X 27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 58%。

力成(6239) 
買進, TP 470 (潛在報酬 101.72%)  
買進 
Price(07/29):233 
T arget Price：470

隨著短期記憶體供需失衡情形難以緩解，加上HBM排擠效應帶來
標準型記憶體外包訂單，力成2026年營運成長根基極為穩固，在
第二季繳出營收創近年新高的優異成績後 ， 受惠於大晶片FCBGA
放量與原物料成本順利轉嫁，第三季及全年逐季成長趨勢已然確
立。更為關鍵的是，鑒於AI晶片算力提升，封裝面積持續突破光
罩極限，傳統12吋晶圓已逐漸無法滿足經濟效益，採用方形載具
的扇出型面板級封裝(FOPLP)成為突破瓶頸的最佳解決方案 ， 力成
斬獲國際大廠AMD的戰略合作，成功驗證業界首款2.5D面板級
EFB互連技術，將導入下一代「Venice」CPU，為因應強勁的客
戶需求，管理層已將全年資本支出上修至500億元，全力擴展
FOPLP產能與晶兆成(TeraPower)的高階測試設備，預計下半年
完成產品認證後，將於2027年上半年進入大規模量產，同時，公
司亦積極切入光通訊領域，與Broadcom於新加坡成立合資公司
發展先進封裝 ， 並利用自身微凸塊技術切入3D Optical Engine封
裝，預計年底開始小量生產，為2028年的CPO市場提前佈局。
整體而言，力成已成功從傳統記憶體封測廠轉型為具備AI高階封
測能力的領軍企業，未來營運方向明確，先進封裝、高階邏輯測試
與記憶體封測三大引擎將共同推升業績爆發 ， 成功切入FOPLP打
入高階AI封裝領域有助於市場對其本益比的重新定價。

 近期投資評等 PTI力成科技(6239.TT)公司簡介 
力成科技(6239.TT)成立於1997年5月，並於2003年4月上櫃掛牌交
易至今，力成科技擁有完整的半導體後段封測能力，足以提供客戶從晶
圓凸塊(Bumping)、晶圓測試(Wafer Sort)、晶圓級封裝(WLCSP)、銲
線封裝(Wire bond Package) 、覆晶封裝(Flip chip Package) 、系統級
封裝(System in Package)、面板級扇出型封裝(Fanout)、3 維矽穿孔
(3DIC TSV)、模組與系統級組裝(System Assembly)、封裝與系統級測
試(Final Test)等服務。前10大股東持股合計36.86%。國內主要封測廠
同業為日月光投控(3711.TT)、京元電子(2449.TT)、頎邦科技(6147.TT)
等。

記憶體與邏輯封裝雙引擎驅動，第二季營運創近年新高 
力成科技(6239.TT)2Q26營收達231.15億元，Q+8.45%/Y+27.99%，

自營商 3.27 0 營運分項均呈現年、季雙增，NAND Flash受惠於AI伺服器對高容量
企業級SSD (eSSD) 的強勁需求爆發，帶動相關封裝與測試訂單出貨
暢旺，營收季增兩成；其次，Logic (邏輯封裝) 方面，高階FCBGA已
進入大量生產階段，加上子公司晶兆成 (TeraPower) 承接AI/HPC一
級客戶的高階測試產能滿載，產能利用率維持高檔；DRAM部分則受惠
於HBM產能排擠效應，引發標準型記憶體封測外溢訂單穩健湧入，此
外，針對原物料如金價和電費的上升，公司已自第一季末與四月起陸續
反映於報價，漲幅介於個位數至雙位數，相關效益已於第二季全面浮
現，藉由產品組合優化對毛利有顯著貢獻，有效支撐毛利率向20%邁
進。綜合以上，研究部預估力成科技(6239.TT)2Q26營業收入為
231.16億元，Q+8.45%/Y+27.99%、毛利率21.76%，
Q+2.32ppt/Y+5.84ppt、營業利益率15.27%，Q+2.32ppt/Y+5.11ppt、
稅後純益22.19億元，Q+20.33%/Y+131.24%、EPS為3.00元。

第三季展望樂觀，各產品線成長動能明確 
展望3Q26，管理層預期營收將在第二季的基礎上持續成長，繳出季增
個位數的表現，且毛利率與EPS亦可望進一步提升。結合重點成長分
項觀察：Logic業務將維持強勁的成長動能，主因公司已具備量產大尺
寸FCBGA能力，並進一步於第三季導入大晶片 (1x reticle Size) 封裝
的量產，大幅強化高精度貼裝與大型面積量產良率；NAND及SSD業
務方面，雖然智慧型手機終端需求可能受記憶體價格上漲影響，但AI
應用帶動的整體SSD後段封測需求仍維持高檔，營運表現穩健；
DRAM部分，記憶體產業持續處於供不應求狀態，客戶持續將晶圓產出
極大化，有利於維持封測整體稼動率。此外，封裝材料成本的持續上升
將適度反映於客戶價格，多數客戶已逐步接受價格調整，預期對第三季
營收及毛利率具備正面支撐作用。綜合以上，研究部預估力成科技
(6239.TT)3Q26營業收入為248.53億元，Q+7.52%/Y+24.46%、毛利
率22.28%、營業利益率16.11%、稅後純益25.00億元，
Q+12.67%/Y+62.60%、EPS為3.38元。

各項業務均保持增長，挑戰歷史新高 
展望2026全年，力成營運將挑戰歷史新高，各營業項目均樂觀看待維
持增長態勢，首先，邏輯業務將打下增長基礎，公司與AMD達成歷史
性技術里程碑，成功驗證業界首款2.5D面板級EFB (Elevated Fanout 
Bridge) 互連技術，並將應用於下一代「Venice」CPU，確立其支援大
規模AI互連系統的實力；同時，與Broadcom於新加坡成立合資公司
投入先進RDL與光學引擎技術，預期將吸引更多ASIC業者導入專

3
案；其次為NAND業務，受惠於AI伺服器儲存架構升級，企業級高容
量eSSD需求爆發，多層數堆疊技術大幅提升了封測ASP；第三為
DRAM業務，除了享受標準型記憶體外包紅利，其TSV互連技術已完
成試產，預計第四季進入生產出貨，為未來HBM製造奠定基礎；最後
為SiP/Module，主要跟隨eSSD模組組裝訂單穩定釋出，整體強勁的
AI應用擴展效應弭平車用與消費性市場復甦遲緩的缺口。綜合以上，研
究部預估力成科技(6239.TT)2026年營業收入957.71億元，
Y+27.81%、毛利率21.76%，Y+4.77ppt、營業利益率15.53%，
Y+4.68ppt、稅後純益94.30億元，Y+70.33%、EPS為12.76元。

資本支出大增擴展FOPLP產能 
在強勁的客戶需求所推動下，管理層預計未來2年資本支出將突破500
億元(2025_190億元)，主要用於AI與HPC晶片測試設備及FOPLP擴
廠與設備添購，初步規劃兩階段取得6k月產能，2H26取得2~3k，
1H27取得3~5K，預計2H26年完成客戶認證，1H27進入量產，此
外，子公司Tera power搭配未來先進封裝布局，也已購置廠房於湖口
工業區，將增加CP、FT測試產能，預計未來兩年添購4至500台測
試機台。

FOPLP需求火熱，添購廠房擴廠緩解產能不足 
現行晶圓級封裝技術雖成熟，但面臨物理尺寸限制，加上圓形特徵，使
晶片封裝的載具使用率(carrier usage rate)偏低，加上隨著AI晶片算力
提升，封裝面積正持續增加(Blackwell_80*80mm、
Rubin_100*100mm、Rubin Ultra_120*120mm、Dojo_200*200mm)，
傳統 12 吋晶圓已逐漸無法滿足需求，因此，採用方形載具的扇出型面
板級封裝(Fanout Panel Level Package；FOPLP)成為新的解決方案，
以達到單次封裝晶片更多，並使單位成本降低；力成的 FOPLP 技術
聚焦於AI Chip、CPU及ASIC市場，並由此延伸進入了光學引擎領
域，目前正在開發及驗證階段，未來將達到光學引擎與AI晶片共同封
裝技術，預計2027年量產FOPLP，並於2028年量產CPO封裝。 
目前FOPLP主要規格將以510*515mm為主，已解決翹曲問題進展到
最終拉升良率階段，目標客群為AI ASIC、CPU客戶，並提供解決方案
包含Bump Free、Chip First、Chip Last、Chip Middle等方案。力成早
已於2019年量產FOPLP技術，初期以Chip First為主，近期因應異
質整合、高精密度線寬線距及多層重布線層(Re-Distribution Layer；
RDL)需求，增加Chip Last生產能力，因此產品線橫跨Chip First與
Chip Last，以因應不同客層的市場需求，現行產能已供不應求，2025

年底向友達購置的廠房預計將於2H26加入營運貢獻，屆時有望將產能
顯著提升，並視需求將持續尋覓未來擴廠基地。綜合以上，研究部預估
力成科技(6239.TT)2027年營業收入1341.47億元，Y+40.07%、毛利
率25.09%，Y+3.33ppt、營業利益率18.84%，Y+3.31ppt、稅後純益
173.15億元，Y+83.62%、EPS為23.43元。

結論與建議 
隨著短期記憶體供需失衡情形難以緩解，加上HBM排擠效應帶來標準
型記憶體外包訂單，力成2026年營運成長根基極為穩固，在第二季繳
出營收創近年新高的優異成績後，受惠於大晶片FCBGA放量與原物料
成本順利轉嫁，第三季及全年逐季成長趨勢已然確立。更為關鍵的是，
鑒於AI晶片算力提升，封裝面積持續突破光罩極限，傳統12吋晶圓已
逐漸無法滿足經濟效益，採用方形載具的扇出型面板級封裝(FOPLP)成
為突破瓶頸的最佳解決方案，力成斬獲國際大廠AMD的戰略合作，成
功驗證業界首款2.5D面板級EFB互連技術，將導入下一代「Venice」
CPU，為因應強勁的客戶需求，管理層已將全年資本支出上修至500
億元，全力擴展FOPLP產能與晶兆成(TeraPower)的高階測試設備，預
計下半年完成產品認證後，將於2027年上半年進入大規模量產，同
時，公司亦積極切入光通訊領域，與Broadcom於新加坡成立合資公司
發展先進封裝，並利用自身微凸塊技術切入3D Optical Engine封裝，
預計年底開始小量生產，為2028年的CPO市場提前佈局。整體而
言，力成已成功從傳統記憶體封測廠轉型為具備AI高階封測能力的領
軍企業，未來營運方向明確，先進封裝、高階邏輯測試與記憶體封測三
大引擎將共同推升業績爆發，成功切入FOPLP打入高階AI封裝領域有
助於市場對其本益比的重新定價。綜合以上，研究部預估力成科技
(6239.TT)2027年營業收入1341.47億元，Y+40.07%、毛利率
25.09%，Y+3.33ppt、營業利益率18.84%，Y+3.31ppt、稅後純益
173.15億元，Y+83.62%、EPS為23.43元。公司近3年本益比區間
為10~40倍，研究部認為未來營運方向明確打下三隻腳，並斬獲國際
指標性客戶之重大戰略合作，有助於評價提升，因此給予20倍本益比
評價，目標價格470元，投資建議為買進。
